140                         // Data Registers

144                         // Custom AXI Debug Registers (in TCK domain)
145                         // CMD format: [31] Write/Read, [30:0] Address
146                         reg [64:0] axi_cmd_shift; // 65-bit shift (1-bit Write, 32-bit Addr, 32-bit Data)

204                         // Clock Domain Crossing (TCK -> System Clock)

439                                     end
440                                     
441                                     REFILL_DATA: begin
442        1/1                              if (rready && rvalid) begin
443        1/1                                  case (word_cnt)
444        1/1                                      3'd0: line_buf[31:0]    <= rdata;
445        1/1                                      3'd1: line_buf[63:32]   <= rdata;
446        1/1                                      3'd2: line_buf[95:64]   <= rdata;
447        1/1                                      3'd3: line_buf[127:96]  <= rdata;
448        1/1                                      3'd4: line_buf[159:128] <= rdata;
449        1/1                                      3'd5: line_buf[191:160] <= rdata;
450        1/1                                      3'd6: line_buf[223:192] <= rdata;
451        1/1                                      3'd7: line_buf[255:224] <= rdata;
                   ==>  MISSING_DEFAULT
452                                             endcase
453        1/1                                  word_cnt <= word_cnt + 1'b1;
454                                             
455        1/1                                  if (rlast) begin
456        1/1                                      rready <= 1'b0;
457                                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
458                                         end
                   ==>  MISSING_ELSE
459                                     end
460                                     
461                                     WRITE_MERGE: begin
462                                         // Write the filled (and optionally merged) line into the cache way
463        1/1                              if (!victim_way) begin
464        1/1                                  data_ram_0[lookup_index] <= new_line;
465        1/1                                  tag_ram_0[lookup_index]  <= {1'b1, req_buf_we, lookup_tag}; // dirty if we wrote
466                                         end else begin
467        0/1     ==>                          data_ram_1[lookup_index] <= new_line;
468        0/1     ==>                          tag_ram_1[lookup_index]  <= {1'b1, req_buf_we, lookup_tag};
469                                         end
470                                         // Update LRU
471        1/1                              lru_ram[lookup_index] <= !victim_way;
472                                         
473        1/1                              req_buf_valid <= 1'b0;
474                                     end
                   ==>  MISSING_DEFAULT
